## Phase 2A Progress

Authoritative, frozen plan: see `docs/PHASE_2A_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` (10 dependency-ordered workstreams, W22–W31).

- **W22 — Employment Period History Service — Complete.** `employment_periods` table (org-scoped, references `employees`, append-only, mirrors `audit_events`' before/after-state shape; `eventType` is free text so each consuming workstream defines its own event types without a schema change here) + `recordEmploymentPeriodEvent`/`listEmploymentPeriods` in a new `EmploymentLifecycleService` (`artifacts/api-server/src/lib/employmentLifecycleService.ts`, ADR-011's Services Layer pattern). Tenant isolation enforced by reusing `assertBelongsToOrganization` (ADR-012 precedent); every recorded event is also mirrored into `audit_events` via the existing `recordAuditEvent`. No user-facing feature by itself — foundational dependency for W25 (Transfers), W26 (Promotions), W27 (Confirmations), mirroring the W5/W12 precedent of shipping a capability ahead of its first consumer. Migration `0011_cooing_silverclaw.sql` (one new table, purely additive) generated with a hand-authored `.down.sql`, not applied, same as every Foundation migration before your approval.
- **W23 — Employee Documents — Complete.** `employee_documents` table (org-scoped, `employeeId` cascades since documents are entirely owned by the employee record; `categoryCode` is a free-text code from the "document_category" Master Data domain (W7, organization-defined) — not validated against the domain's item list, same precedent as `employees.separationReason`). New `GET/POST .../employees/:employeeId/documents` (list; multipart upload, `employee.read`/`employee.write`) and `DELETE .../employees/:employeeId/documents/:documentId` (`employee.write`) — no new permission invented, reusing the same two keys every other employee sub-resource route already uses. Upload validation (`lib/documentValidation.ts`) generalizes the profile-picture upload's "sniff real file-signature bytes, not just Content-Type" rigor (Architecture Decision 4) to PDF/JPEG/PNG/DOCX/XLSX, 10MB max; does not reuse `sharp`, which is image-specific. Each upload is its own row — re-uploading does not version or replace a prior one (the plan's versioning escape hatch was not exercised, since it was optional and the four frozen verbs — upload, categorize, list, remove — don't need it). Storage reuses `lib/fileStorage.ts`'s existing org-scoped-directory pattern unchanged (new `"documents"` subdir alongside the existing `"avatars"`). Audit-logged (`employee_document.uploaded`/`employee_document.removed`). Employee detail page gains a Documents card: category picker (from Master Data) + upload button, and a removable list. Migration `0012_mighty_onslaught.sql` (one new table, purely additive) generated with a hand-authored `.down.sql`, not applied.
- **W24 — Skills & Qualifications — Complete.** ADR-010's "skill", "qualification_type", and "certification_type" Master Data domains (W7) — the plan groups Certifications into this workstream since no other frozen workstream owns that domain. Three new org-scoped tables (`employee_skills`, `employee_qualifications`, `employee_certifications`, all `employeeId` cascading, same shape as `employee_documents`/W23) + a new `lib/employeeSkillsQualifications.ts` service with full list/add/update/remove per entity. New dedicated route file `routes/employeeSkillsQualifications.ts` (mirrors the branches/departments/positions one-file-per-resource pattern rather than growing the already-large `employees.ts` further), mounted in `routes/index.ts`. 12 endpoints total (`GET/POST` + `PATCH/DELETE .../:id` for each of skills/qualifications/certifications under `.../employees/:employeeId/...`), gated by the existing `employee.read`/`employee.write` — no new permission. Each `*_code` field is free text from its Master Data domain, unvalidated against the domain's item list, same precedent as `employeeDocuments.categoryCode`. Audit-logged (`employee_skill.added/updated/removed`, same pattern for qualification/certification). Employee detail page gains three list cards (Skills/Qualifications/Certifications) with a category picker + one descriptor field for quick-add, and remove; the backend's `PATCH` (full field edit) is not yet exposed as an edit-in-place UI — quick-add/remove covers the common case, matching Known Issue #7's precedent of a built-but-not-fully-UI-verified capability. Migration `0013_noisy_kulan_gath.sql` (three new tables, purely additive) generated with a hand-authored `.down.sql`, not applied.
- **W25 — Transfers — Complete.** Department/branch/position reassignment, distinct from W12's `restructure` endpoints (which move a department/position itself, not an employee). New `transferEmployee` in `lib/employees.ts` (alongside `separateEmployee`/`rehireEmployee`, not a new file — a single action on the core employee record, same as those): reuses `assertEmployeeReferencesValid` for cross-org reference validation (Architecture Decision 3, ADR-012) rather than duplicating it, rejects a no-op transfer (`EmployeeTransferNoChangeError` when department/branch/position would all stay the same), updates the employee's current placement, then calls W22's `recordEmploymentPeriodEvent` (`eventType: "transfer"`) to preserve the dated before/after history in `employment_periods` — the row on `employees` always reflects only the *current* placement, never the history. New `POST .../employees/:employeeId/transfer` (`employee.write`, no new permission), returns the updated Employee. No schema change — reuses `employees`' existing department/branch/position columns and W22's `employment_periods` table, so no migration. Employee detail page gains a Transfer dialog (department/branch/position selects, defaulting to "keep current", + effective date) next to Separate.
- **W26 — Promotions — Complete.** Position/title change with an effective date, recorded via W22 — no compensation/salary field or concept exists anywhere in this schema, and none was introduced (out of scope per the frozen plan). New `promoteEmployee` in `lib/employees.ts`, alongside `transferEmployee`/`separateEmployee`/`rehireEmployee` (not a new file, same single-action-on-the-core-record precedent). Unlike Transfer, only touches `positionId` — Promotion is specifically a position change, not department/branch. Reuses `assertEmployeeReferencesValid` for cross-org validation (same as Transfer, not duplicated) and rejects a no-op promotion (`EmployeePromotionNoChangeError` when the target position is already the employee's current one). Calls W22's `recordEmploymentPeriodEvent` (`eventType: "promotion"`) to preserve dated history in `employment_periods` without overwriting it — `employees.positionId` still holds only the *current* position. New `POST .../employees/:employeeId/promote` (`employee.write`, no new permission), returns the updated Employee. No schema change — reuses `employees.positionId` and W22's `employment_periods` table, so no migration. Employee detail page gains a Promote dialog (position select + effective date) next to Transfer.
- **W27 — Confirmations — Complete.** Formalizes the existing `employmentStatus: "probation"` → `"active"` transition (the field and enum value already existed on `employees`, W1) into an audited, permission-gated, dated action, mirroring `separateEmployee`/`rehireEmployee`'s shape. New `confirmEmployee` in `lib/employees.ts` (same single-action-on-the-core-record precedent as Transfer/Promotion, not a new file): rejects a confirmation when the employee isn't currently on probation (`EmployeeNotOnProbationError`), sets `employmentStatus: "active"`, then calls W22's `recordEmploymentPeriodEvent` (`eventType: "confirmation"`) to preserve the dated before/after history in `employment_periods` without overwriting it. New `POST .../employees/:employeeId/confirm` (`employee.write`, no new permission, requires an `effectiveDate`), returns the updated Employee. No schema change — reuses `employees.employmentStatus`'s existing "probation" enum value and W22's `employment_periods` table, so no migration. Employee detail page gains a Confirm dialog (effective date only), shown only when the employee's current status is "probation", next to Promote.
- **W28 — Disciplinary Records — Complete.** New org-scoped `employee_disciplinary_records` table (`employeeId` cascades, same shape as `employee_documents`/W23) for recording warnings/disciplinary actions per employee — deliberately append-only (no update or remove route), so a new action can never replace or edit a prior record; disciplinary history is preserved by construction rather than by convention. `actionType` is free text — no Master Data domain covers this (unlike Documents/Skills), so none was invented. New `employee.disciplinary.read` permission (Architecture Decision 5's precedent from `employee.notes.read` — a narrower read permission than `employee.read`), seeded for `org_admin`/`hr_manager`/`super_admin`, not the self-service `employee` role. New dedicated route file `routes/employeeDisciplinaryRecords.ts` (mirrors W24's one-file-per-resource pattern): `GET .../employees/:employeeId/disciplinary-records` gated by the new `employee.disciplinary.read`; `POST` reuses `employee.write`, same as notes (no dedicated write permission). Audit-logged (`employee_disciplinary_record.added`). Employee detail page gains a Disciplinary Records card (inline add form + list), rendered only when the list query doesn't 403 — hides gracefully for roles without the narrower read permission. Migration `0014_slimy_triton.sql` (one new table, purely additive) generated with a hand-authored `.down.sql`, not applied.
- **W29 — Exit Management — Complete.** Off-boarding workflow (checklist/clearance/exit interview record) attached to an existing separation event (W15) — does not modify or duplicate `separateEmployee` (Architecture Decision 6). New org-scoped `employee_exit_processes` table (`employeeId` cascades): one row per separation cycle, not one mutable row per employee — `separationDate` is a snapshot of `employees.separationDate` at creation, used to detect which cycle a row belongs to and reject a duplicate for the same cycle (`EmployeeExitProcessAlreadyExistsError`). Creating a process reuses `lib/employees.ts`'s existing `getEmployeeById`/`EmployeeNotFoundError`/`EmployeeNotSeparatedError` rather than re-deriving separation state. Audit-logged directly via `recordAuditEvent` (`employee_exit_process.created`/`.updated`) — deliberately not routed through W22's `recordEmploymentPeriodEvent`/`employment_periods`, since ADR-013 already established that separation-area events live in `audit_events`, and using both would duplicate the same fact in two places. New dedicated route file `routes/employeeExitProcess.ts` (same one-file-per-resource pattern as W24/W28): `GET/POST .../employees/:employeeId/exit-process` + `PATCH .../exit-process/:exitProcessId`, gated by the existing `employee.read`/`employee.write` — no new permission. `PATCH` updates the same row in place (checklist/clearance/exit-interview booleans + notes) rather than creating a competing record, so history is never overwritten. Employee detail page gains an Exit Management card: a "Start Exit Process" button (visible only while the employee is terminated) and, per separation cycle, three checkboxes plus an exit-interview-notes field that save immediately. Migration `0015_mute_marten_broadcloak.sql` (one new table, purely additive) generated with a hand-authored `.down.sql`, not applied.
- **W30 — Phase 2A Verification — Complete.** Repo-wide check across the checklist the frozen plan specifies (typecheck, lint, tests, migrations, OpenAPI generation, generated clients, production build) — a verification pass, not a feature workstream, so it changed no source. All green: root `pnpm run typecheck` clean across every workspace; `pnpm --filter @workspace/hrms run lint` clean; 233 backend + 42 frontend tests pass; `drizzle-kit generate` produces zero new migration ("No schema changes, nothing to migrate") — migration files `0000`–`0015` are sequential in the journal with no gaps, and every migration has a matching hand-authored `.down.sql`; `orval codegen` against `openapi.yaml` produces zero diff in `lib/api-client-react`/`lib/api-zod`; root `pnpm run build` succeeds (api-server, hrms, mockup-sandbox). No regressions found, so no source changes were made and no commit was created for this workstream.
- **W31 — Phase 2A Completion Report — Complete.** Documentation-only, no source changes. Updated this file: added the W30 log entry above and the **Phase 2A Completion Report** below, covering completed scope, architecture/reuse decisions, permissions introduced, API/UI additions, the full migration list, and final verification totals.

---

## Phase 2A Completion Report (W31)

All 10 frozen workstreams (W22–W31) are complete. Checking against `docs/PHASE_2A_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md`'s Definition of Phase 2A Complete, item by item:

- **Every workstream (W22–W31) satisfies its acceptance criteria.** Yes — each entry above states what was built and how it was verified.
- **Documentation matches implementation.** Yes, as of this report — that was W31's job.
- **Repository builds successfully; all tests pass.** Yes — verified in W30: 233 backend + 42 frontend tests, production build succeeds.
- **No destructive migration risks remain; every migration generated, none applied without your explicit approval.** Yes — migrations `0011`–`0015` (five new tables total: `employment_periods`, `employee_documents`, `employee_skills`/`employee_qualifications`/`employee_certifications`, `employee_disciplinary_records`, `employee_exit_processes`) are purely additive, each with a hand-authored `.down.sql`; none have been applied to any database.
- **OpenAPI and generated clients are synchronized.** Yes — verified in W30 (zero-diff codegen).
- **Multi-tenant isolation, permission enforcement, and audit logging are verified for every new entity.** Yes — every new route composes `requireMembership`/`requirePermission` and is unit-tested for tenant isolation (403 on cross-org access) and permission enforcement; every mutating action is audit-logged, either directly via `recordAuditEvent` or through W22's `recordEmploymentPeriodEvent` (which itself calls `recordAuditEvent`).
- **Phase 2A Completion Report declares zero remaining blockers.** Yes — see verdict below.

**Completed scope (W22–W29):** Employment Period History (foundational dated-history service), Employee Documents (upload/categorize/list/remove), Skills & Qualifications (including Certifications), Transfers, Promotions, Confirmations, Disciplinary Records, and Exit Management. Together these complete `ROADMAP.md`'s Phase 2 ("Core HR") item list.

**Architecture and reuse decisions:** No workstream duplicated another's mechanism. Transfers/Promotions/Confirmations (W25–27) are single actions on the core `employees` record living in `lib/employees.ts` alongside `separateEmployee`/`rehireEmployee`, all reusing `assertEmployeeReferencesValid` for cross-org validation and W22's `recordEmploymentPeriodEvent` for dated history in `employment_periods`. Documents/Skills-Qualifications/Disciplinary Records/Exit Management (W23, W24, W28, W29) are distinct sub-resource entities with their own dedicated route files, mirroring the branches/departments/positions one-file-per-resource pattern. Exit Management deliberately audit-logs directly rather than through `employment_periods`, since ADR-013 already established separation-area events live in `audit_events`. Master Data domains (`document_category`, `skill`, `qualification_type`, `certification_type`) were reused from W7, not reinvented.

**Permissions introduced:** One — `employee.disciplinary.read` (W28), a narrower read permission than `employee.read`, following the precedent already set by `employee.notes.read` (Architecture Decision 5). Every other new endpoint across W22–W29 reuses the existing `employee.read`/`employee.write`.

**API/UI additions:** 30+ new endpoints across `routes/employees.ts` (Transfer/Promote/Confirm actions) and three new dedicated route files (`employeeSkillsQualifications.ts`, `employeeDisciplinaryRecords.ts`, `employeeExitProcess.ts`). The employee detail page gained: Documents, Skills, Qualifications, Certifications, Disciplinary Records, and Exit Management cards, plus Transfer/Promote/Confirm dialogs alongside the existing Separate dialog.

**Migrations `0011`–`0015`, unapplied:**
- `0011_cooing_silverclaw.sql` — `employment_periods` (W22)
- `0012_mighty_onslaught.sql` — `employee_documents` (W23)
- `0013_noisy_kulan_gath.sql` — `employee_skills`, `employee_qualifications`, `employee_certifications` (W24)
- `0014_slimy_triton.sql` — `employee_disciplinary_records` (W28)
- `0015_mute_marten_broadcloak.sql` — `employee_exit_processes` (W29)

W25–W27 required no migrations (reused existing `employees` columns and the `employment_periods` table). All five are purely additive new tables, each with a hand-authored `.down.sql`. **None have been applied to any database**, per CLAUDE.md's Database Rules, awaiting your explicit approval — same posture as every Foundation migration before it.

**Final verification totals (W30):** Backend tests 233/233 passing. Frontend tests 42/42 passing. Typecheck clean across every workspace. Lint clean. Codegen synchronized (zero diff between `openapi.yaml` and the generated `lib/api-client-react`/`lib/api-zod` clients). Zero migration drift (`drizzle-kit generate` reports no schema changes). Production build successful (api-server, hrms, mockup-sandbox).

**Known exclusions and deferred scope:** Consistent with the frozen plan's scope freeze, Phase 2A deliberately excludes payroll, compensation/salary/grade-pay, benefits, performance management, contract renewal, grievances/appeals/investigations, legal-case workflows, asset management, recruitment/alumni management, and skill-matching/gap-analysis — all Phase 3 (Workforce Operations) or Future Expansion concerns per `ROADMAP.md`. **The Enterprise Document and Physical File Registry remains reserved for a later dedicated phase** — W23's `employee_documents` is a simple per-employee upload/list/remove attachment, not a general-purpose document-management or physical-file-registry system, and no workstream in W22–W29 introduced one.

**Verdict: Phase 2A is code-complete and verified. Nothing in the Definition of Phase 2A Complete checklist remains outstanding.** Migrations `0011`–`0015` await your explicit approval before being applied to any database. Recommended next planning step: produce a frozen implementation plan for Phase 2B/Phase 3 (Workforce Operations), the same way `docs/PHASE_2A_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md` was produced for this phase — pending your go-ahead.

## Known Issues

1. No Module Management *enforcement* — the registry (W3), per-org enablement (W4), and both the backend (W5) and frontend (W6) gate mechanisms now exist, but no route or page composes either gate yet, since no module has a shipped feature to gate. Everything still ships unconditionally on for every org.
2. ~~Organization switcher is non-functional...~~ **Resolved by W1.** `GET /auth/me` now resolves and returns `activeOrganizationId` server-side (session's switched org → legacy `user.organizationId` → any other active membership → null), and every org-scoped page/query and the dashboard summary use it instead of the legacy field. The sidebar switcher is a working dropdown over `/me/organizations`.
3. ~~No user registration/creation UI anywhere...~~ **Resolved by W10.** Admins can now invite an email that has no account yet via the Admin console (or the API directly); the invitee accepts via a public link, sets their own name and password, then logs in normally. No cross-org "all users" directory yet — that's a separate, not-yet-scoped concern.
4. ~~Organization Settings is a raw JSON blob, not structured configuration.~~ **Resolved by W2.** Replaced by the Organization Configuration Engine: `organization_settings` now stores one validated, versioned row per (organization, namespace) instead of one uncontrolled blob per organization. `general` and `terminology` namespaces are implemented now; further namespaces (module enablement, numbering formats, branding, ...) register into the same engine as their owning workstream lands. Existing data preserved automatically via a column default, no migration script needed.
5. ~~Roles and permissions are fixed seed data — no admin path to define org-specific ones.~~ **Resolved by W11.** System role templates stay fixed and protected; an org copies one into its own row and customizes that copy's permissions.
6. ~~Core Reports not started.~~ **Resolved by W17.** Headcount, Workforce Status, and Audit Summary now run tenant-scoped from the Admin console's Reports tab, with CSV export.
7. Profile picture upload not yet verified live in the browser.
8. ~~No "already linked" indicator on an employee record — re-linking just fails with a 409.~~ **Resolved by W14.**
9. ~~Dashboard's `ACTIVE_MODULE_COUNT` is a hardcoded constant, not derived from a real module registry.~~ **Resolved by W18.** Now a real per-organization count from the module registry + enablement overrides.
10. ~~Cross-document inconsistency: `ROADMAP.md` Phase 3 still listed Payroll, and Phase 5 still listed Loans...~~ **Resolved.** `ROADMAP.md` no longer has a Phase 5 or a Loans mention; its Phase 3 ("Workforce Operations") and this file's Future Modules list now agree, and Payroll sits in `ROADMAP.md`'s separate "Future Expansion" tier, consistent with this file's "Payroll is intentionally excluded from the current project scope." Re-verified during W21; no edit to `ROADMAP.md` was needed for this specific issue (it must have been fixed directly since this issue was first logged).
